                                         CHAPTER 5

                     FIELD MAINTENANCE INSTRUCTIONS

    Note. This chapter contains information for field maintenance personnel. The amount of repair that can be per-
formed by units having field and depot maintenance responsibility is limited only by the tools and test equipment
available, and by the skill of the repairman.

Section |. TROUBLE SHOOTING AT FIELD MAINTENANCE LEVEL

    Warning: Be extremely careful when servicing the receiver; dangerous      high voltages are
present. When checking voltages, use probes that are completely insulated    except for the tip.
Observe polarities to protect the meter. Take no continuity readings unless  the receiver power
is removed. Discharge capacitors before checking.

60. Trouble-Shooting Procedures                                might otherwise occur as a result of
                                                               improper servicing, and in forestall-
    a. The first step in servicing a set is to at-             ing future failures.
tempt to sectionalize the fault. Sectionalizing
means tracing the fault to the major component,          (2) Input resistance measurements. These
circuit, or stage in the receiver responsible for
abnormal operation of the set: The second step                 measurements prevent further dam-
is to localize the fault. Localization means trac-              age to the receiver from possible short
ing the fault to the defective part responsible                 circuits. Since this test gives an indi-
for the abnormal condition. Some troubles such                 cation of the condition of the filter cir-
as burned-out resistors, r-f arcing, and shorted                cuits, its function can be considered as
transformers can be located by sight, smell, or                being more than preventive.
hearing. The majority of faults must, however,
be localized by checking the voltage and re-             (3) Operational tests. The operational
sistance.
                                                               test is important because it frequently
   b. The tests listed below aid in isolating the               indicates the general location of the
